Folio 0.20.0 — 오픈소스 라이선스 고지 (Third-Party Notices)
========================================================================

본 제품은 한컴의 HWP 문서 파일(.hwp) 공개 문서를 참고하여 개발하였습니다.

Folio는 아래 오픈소스 소프트웨어를 포함합니다. 각 구성요소는 원래의 라이선스를 따르며,
Folio 고유 코드에 대한 권리가 이 구성요소들에 적용되지 않습니다.

[웹 뷰어 구성요소]
  - @cantoo/pdf-lib 2.11.1 — MIT
  - @noble/ciphers 2.4.0 — MIT
  - @rhwp/core 0.8.7 — MIT
  - culori 4.0.2 — MIT
  - docx-preview 0.4.1 — Apache-2.0
  - echarts 6.1.0 — Apache-2.0
  - tslib 2.3.0 — 0BSD
  - fflate 0.8.3 — MIT
  - html-entities 2.6.0 — MIT
  - jszip 3.10.2 — (MIT OR GPL-3.0-or-later)
  - lie 3.3.0 — MIT
  - immediate 3.0.6 — MIT
  - setimmediate 1.0.5 — MIT
  - libarchive.js 2.0.2 — MIT
  - libheif-js 1.23.5 — LGPL-3.0
  - lodash 4.18.1 — MIT
  - marked 18.1.1 — MIT
  - node-html-better-parser 1.5.9 — MIT
  - pako 1.0.11 — (MIT AND Zlib)
  - pdfjs-dist 6.5.438 — Apache-2.0
  - postal-mime 4.1.4 — MIT-0
  - pptx-preview 1.0.7 — ISC
  - regenerator-runtime 0.13.11 — MIT
  - tesseract.js 7.0.0 — Apache-2.0
  - tslib 2.9.1 — 0BSD
  - utif 3.1.0 — MIT
  - uuid 11.1.1 — MIT
  - xlsx 0.20.3 — Apache-2.0
  - zrender 6.1.0 — BSD-3-Clause
  - core-js 3.51.0 — MIT
  - Brotli decoder pdf.js 포함본 — MIT
  - libarchive 3.7.2 — BSD-2-Clause
  - tesseract.js-core 7.0.0 — Apache-2.0
  - bergamot-translator 0.4.9 — MPL-2.0
  - Firefox Translations models ko · ja · zh ⇄ en — MPL-2.0
  - Liberation Fonts 2.1.5 — OFL-1.1
  - rhwp_bg.wasm 안의 Rust 구성요소(Rust 표준 라이브러리 포함) — 아래 "한글 엔진 내부 구성요소" 참고

                            Preamble

  The GNU General Public License is a free, copyleft license for
software and other kinds of works.

  The licenses for most software and other practical works are designed
to take away your freedom to share and change the works.  By contrast,
the GNU General Public License is intended to guarantee your freedom to
share and change all versions of a program--to make sure it remains free
software for all its users.  We, the Free Software Foundation, use the
GNU General Public License for most of our software; it applies also to
any other work released this way by its authors.  You can apply it to
your programs, too.

  When we speak of free software, we are referring to freedom, not
price.  Our General Public Licenses are designed to make sure that you
have the freedom to distribute copies of free software (and charge for
them if you wish), that you receive source code or can get it if you
want it, that you can change the software or use pieces of it in new
free programs, and that you know you can do these things.

  To protect your rights, we need to prevent others from denying you
these rights or asking you to surrender the rights.  Therefore, you have
certain responsibilities if you distribute copies of the software, or if
you modify it: responsibilities to respect the freedom of others.

  For example, if you distribute copies of such a program, whether
gratis or for a fee, you must pass on to the recipients the same
freedoms that you received.  You must make sure that they, too, receive
or can get the source code.  And you must show them these terms so they
know their rights.

  Developers that use the GNU GPL protect your rights with two steps:
(1) assert copyright on the software, and (2) offer you this License
giving you legal permission to copy, distribute and/or modify it.

  For the developers' and authors' protection, the GPL clearly explains
that there is no warranty for this free software.  For both users' and
authors' sake, the GPL requires that modified versions be marked as
changed, so that their problems will not be attributed erroneously to
authors of previous versions.

  Some devices are designed to deny users access to install or run
modified versions of the software inside them, although the manufacturer
can do so.  This is fundamentally incompatible with the aim of
protecting users' freedom to change the software.  The systematic
pattern of such abuse occurs in the area of products for individuals to
use, which is precisely where it is most unacceptable.  Therefore, we
have designed this version of the GPL to prohibit the practice for those
products.  If such problems arise substantially in other domains, we
stand ready to extend this provision to those domains in future versions
of the GPL, as needed to protect the freedom of users.

  Finally, every program is threatened constantly by software patents.
States should not allow patents to restrict development and use of
software on general-purpose computers, but in those that do, we wish to
avoid the special danger that patents applied to a free program could
make it effectively proprietary.  To prevent this, the GPL assures that
patents cannot be used to render the program non-free.

  The precise terms and conditions for copying, distribution and
modification follow.

pptx-preview 1.0.7 (ISC) — 微信：_hit757_

라이선스 표기에 관한 사실
- package.json의 "license" 필드: ISC
- 패키지 안에 별도의 라이선스 파일은 없음
- 작성자 README 요약: npm 패키지는 개인·상업 용도로 무료 사용 가능. 소스 코드는 개인 학습용이며 인터넷 플랫폼에 게시할 수 없고, 직접 수정해 자기 프로젝트로 오픈소스화할 수 없음
- 작성자 표기: 微信 _hit757_ (https://www.npmjs.com/package/pptx-preview)

Folio는 npm 배포본을 사용하되, 슬라이드와 그림이 사라지는 오류를 막고 줄 간격, 문단 여백, 차트 기본 제목을
조정하기 위해 빌드할 때 배포 파일의 몇 곳을 수정해 압축된 형태로만 포함합니다. 이 라이브러리의 원본 소스
코드를 포함하거나 공개하지 않고, 다른 라이선스로 재배포하지 않습니다.

아래는 package.json이 가리키는 ISC 라이선스의 표준 문구입니다.

Copyright (c) pptx-preview 작성자 (npm 패키지 작성자 표기: 微信 _hit757_)

Permission to use, copy, modify, and/or distribute this software for any purpose with or
without fee is hereby granted, provided that the above copyright notice and this permission

THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS" AND THE AUTHOR DISCLAIMS ALL WARRANTIES WITH REGARD TO THIS
SOFTWARE INCLUDING ALL IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE
AUTHOR BE LIABLE FOR ANY SPECIAL, DIRECT, INDIRECT, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES OR ANY DAMAGES
WHATSOEVER RESULTING FROM LOSS OF USE, DATA OR PROFITS, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT,

PERFORMANCE OF THIS SOFTWARE.

한글 엔진 내부 구성요소 (rhwp v0.8.7)

한글 엔진(rhwp_bg.wasm)은 Rust로 만들어졌어요. 엔진에 항상 들어가는 Rust 표준 라이브러리와, rhwp의 의존성 목록(Cargo.lock)에 있는 Rust 라이브러리 374개의 저작권 문구와 라이선스 전문을 싣습니다. 이 목록에는 빌드 도구나 다른 플랫폼 전용 라이브러리처럼 실제 엔진에는 들어가지 않는 것도 넉넉히 포함되어 있어요. 패키지에 라이선스 파일이 없어 표기된 라이선스를 따르는 라이브러리: defmt-parser 1.0.0 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), gl_generator 0.14.0 (Apache-2.0), gpu-descriptor 0.3.2 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), gpu-descriptor-types 0.2.0 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), hexf-parse 0.2.1 (CC0-1.0), jni-sys-macros 0.4.1 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), khronos_api 3.1.0 (Apache-2.0), ndk-sys 0.6.0+11769913 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), profiling 1.0.18 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), r-efi 5.3.0 (MIT OR Apache-2.0 OR LGPL-2.1-or-later), r-efi 6.0.0 (MIT OR Apache-2.0 OR LGPL-2.1-or-later), skia-bindings 0.153.3 (MIT), skia-safe 0.153.3 (MIT), spirv 0.4.0+sdk-1.4.341.0 (Apache-2.0), subsecond 0.7.10 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), subsecond-types 0.7.10 (MIT OR Apache-2.0), svg_fmt 0.4.5 (MIT/Apache-2.0), zune-core 0.4.12 (MIT OR Apache-2.0 OR Zlib), zune-jpeg 0.4.21 (MIT OR Apache-2.0 OR Zlib).

# Code

ttf-parser
Code from ttf-parser was used/adapted for the following parts:
- Reader, Writer, LazyArray16 and LazyArray16Iter
- `name` table parsing.
- `hmtx` table parsing.
- Most of the `cff` parsing logic as well auxiliary structs and functions
such as `calc_subroutine_bias`, `SIDMetadata`, `CIDMetadata` `RealNumber`,
`Index`, `IndexSize`, `OffsetSize`, `FDSelect`, `ArgumentStack`, `Top Dict` and `FONT Dict`,
`DictionaryParser`.
